vs. Horiguchi]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Freedom 250''''' (also known as '''''UFC White House''''' and '''''UFC at the White House'''''){{Cite web|url=https://www.paramountplus.com/sneak-peak/where-to-watch-white-house-ufc/|title=UFC at the White House: Where to watch|publisher=paramountplus.com|author=Christopher Cason|date=2026-05-15|access-date=2026-05-17|archive-date=May 21, 2026|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260521174342/https://www.paramountplus.com/sneak-peak/where-to-watch-white-house-ufc|url-status=live}} was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on June 14, 2026, on the [[South Lawn]] of the [[White House]] in [[Washington, D.C.]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/ufc-ceo-dana-white-confirms-event-at-white-house-is-on-after-meeting-with-trump-administration/|title=UFC CEO Dana White confirms event at White House is on after meeting with Trump administration|publisher=cbssports.com|author=Shakiel Mahjouri|date=2025-08-29|access-date=2025-08-31|archive-date=September 6, 2025|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250906180654/https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/ufc-ceo-dana-white-confirms-event-at-white-house-is-on-after-meeting-with-trump-administration/|url-status=live}}{{Cite web|url=https://thehill.com/homenews/administration/5488203-white-house-ufc-cage-match-2026/|title=White House UFC event set for June, weigh-ins at Lincoln Memorial|publisher=thehill.com|author=Elizabeth Crisp|date=2025-09-05|access-date=2025-09-05|archive-date=September 13, 2025|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250913221237/https://thehill.com/homenews/administration/5488203-white-house-ufc-cage-match-2026/|url-status=live}} The event's name is a reference to the [[United States Semiquincentennial|250th anniversary of the United States Declaration of Independence]] on the following July 4, 20 days later.{{Cite web|url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/mixed-martial-arts/articles/c743r0kl4wjo|title=Pereira to fight for third title at UFC White House|publisher=bbc.com|author=Paul Battison|date=2026-03-08|access-date=2026-03-08}}\n\nThere was seating for roughly 4,300 people.{{Cite web |title=Everything to know about the UFC White House centerpiece: The claw |url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/48946908/ufc-freedom-250-white-house-claw |access-date=2026-07-11 |website=www.espn.com |date=June 10, 2026 }} No public seating was made available, with seats reserved for financial sponsors, political allies, and active-duty service members. The event marked the [[List of UFC records#Highest percentage of knockouts - In a single event|only UFC event to end with all fights ending by knockout]], and also tied events [[UFC Fight Night: Rockhold vs. Bisping]] and [[UFC on ESPN: Santos vs. Hill]] for having [[List of UFC records#Highest percentage of finishes - In a single event|all fights end by finish at a modern-era UFC event]], which had 11 and 10 fights respectively. \n\nThe event was the most-watched exclusive live event on Paramount+ {{As of|2026|June|lc=y}}. It had 34 million viewers worldwide and was one of the most watched MMA events of all time. There was controversy surrounding the event's use of the White House to stage cage fighting, with most Americans disapproving of the idea according to a poll by [[Reuters]]/[[Ipsos]].\n\n==Background==\n[[File:2020 Fall Garden Tours (50506093376).jpg|thumb|left|The [[South Lawn]] of the [[White House]] held the first professional sporting event staged at the presidential residence.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/401228/dana-white-ufc-white-house-event-is-on|title=Dana White: UFC White House event 'is on'|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2025-08-29|access-date=2025-08-31|archive-date=August 30, 2025|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250830034748/https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/401228/dana-white-ufc-white-house-event-is-on|url-status=live}}]]\n\nTo commemorate the [[United States Semiquincentennial|250th anniversary of the United States]], the UFC planned to hold a mixed martial arts event on the South Lawn of the White House in June 2026. \n\nThe event was first announced by President [[Donald Trump]] on July 3, 2025, during a rally at the [[Iowa State Fairgrounds]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2025/7/3/24461432/president-trump-reveals-plans-to-hold-a-ufc-event-at-the-white-house-in-2026|title=President Trump reveals plans to hold a UFC event at the White House in 2026|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2025-07-04|access-date=2025-08-31|archive-date=September 24, 2025|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250924205735/https://www.mmafighting.com/2025/7/3/24461432/president-trump-reveals-plans-to-hold-a-ufc-event-at-the-white-house-in-2026|url-status=live}} On August 29, 2025, UFC CEO [[Dana White]] confirmed that plans for the event had been finalized, stating: \"We had the meeting at the White House ... The White House fight is on.\"{{Cite web|url=https://sports.yahoo.com/mma/breaking-news/article/ufc-ceo-dana-white-says-white-house-card-is-officially-happening-after-meeting-with-trump-administration-035403534.html?guccounter=1|title=UFC CEO Dana White says White House card is officially happening after meeting with Trump administration|publisher=sports.yahoo.com|author=Jack Baer|date=2025-08-31|access-date=2025-08-31|archive-date=February 28, 2026|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260228111249/https://sports.yahoo.com/mma/breaking-news/article/ufc-ceo-dana-white-says-white-house-card-is-officially-happening-after-meeting-with-trump-administration-035403534.html?guccounter=1|url-status=live}} The announcement followed Trump's remarks at the rally, where he referenced his long-standing friendship with White. Trump had frequently attended UFC events in recent years, including [[UFC 316]] in New Jersey on June 7, 2025, where several fighters greeted him cageside. \n\nWhile initial reports suggested a [[4th of July]] date, the event was officially rescheduled for June due to logistical reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.foxnews.com/sports/ufc-white-house-plans-revealed-including-weigh-ins-lincoln-memorial-report|title=UFC White House plans revealed, including weigh-ins at Lincoln Memorial: report|publisher=foxnews.com|author=Ryan Morik|date=2025-09-05|access-date=2025-09-05|archive-date=April 30, 2026|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260430183752/https://www.foxnews.com/sports/ufc-white-house-plans-revealed-including-weigh-ins-lincoln-memorial-report|url-status=live}} During a speech at [[Naval Station Norfolk]] on October 6, 2025, Trump announced that it would take place on June 14, 2026, coinciding with his 80th birthday and the [[Flag Day (United States)|Flag Day]] holiday.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/10/06/donald-trump-ufc-white-house-event-date-birthday/86548457007/|title=President Trump reveals UFC White House event will happen on his 80th birthday|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2025-10-06|access-date=2025-10-06|archive-date=December 27, 2025|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20251227222140/https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/10/06/donald-trump-ufc-white-house-event-date-birthday/86548457007/|url-status=live}} The rescheduled date drew criticism over its occurrence on Trump's birthday three weeks before July 4, with several news outlets describing it as celebrating Trump's birthday more than America's.{{Cite news|last=Gelston|first=Dan|title=UFC brings its trademark mayhem to the White House as President Trump celebrates 80th birthday|publisher=[[Associated Press]]|url=https://apnews.com/article/trump-80th-ufc-white-house-724c875d7a7cbfed087e179e8f689ec0|language=en-US|url-status=live|date=June 15, 2026|access-date=November 7, 2026|archive-date=June 15, 2026|access-date=November 7, 2026}}{{Cite news|last=Ramaswamy|first=Swapna Venugopal|date=June 15, 2026|title=Trump's UFC White House birthday bash was a bloody, on-brand affair|work=USA Today|url=https://www.usatoday.com/story/news/politics/2026/06/15/trump-ufc-birthday-white-house/90550948007/|access-date=June 26, 2026}}{{Cite news|last=Gedeon|first=Joseph|date=June 15, stop UFC fight at White House on Trump's birthday|work=CBS News|url=https://www.cbsnews.com/news/white-house-ufc-fight-lawsuit-trumps-birthday-june-14/|access-date=June 10, 2026}}\n\nThe event represented the UFC's third visit to Washington, D.C., and first since [[UFC on ESPN: Overeem vs. Rozenstruik]] in 2019.{{Cite web|url=https://bleacherreport.com/articles/25227329-dana-white-says-badass-ufc-white-house-event-will-be-1-1-experience-video|title=Dana White Says 'Badass' UFC White House Event Will be 'a 1-of-1 weigh-ins took place at the [[Lincoln Memorial]]. The card aired on [[Paramount+]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/461166/dana-white-reveals-broadcast-plans-for-ufc-white-house-card|title=Dana White reveals broadcast plans for UFC White House card|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2025-12-12|access-date=2025-12-12|archive-date=December 12, 2025|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20251212194631/https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/461166/dana-white-reveals-broadcast-plans-for-ufc-white-house-card|url-status=live}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/06/07/ufc-freedom-250-commentary-team-joe-rogan-white-house-broadcast/90441030007/|title=UFC Freedom 250 commentary team: Joe Rogan on call at the White House|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2026-06-07|access-date=2026-06-07|archive-date=June 8, 2026|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260608023530/https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/06/07/ufc-freedom-250-commentary-team-joe-rogan-white-house-broadcast/90441030007/|url-status=live}} The event name, UFC Freedom 250, was announced during the [[UFC 326]] broadcast on March 7, 2026.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/48138882/ufc-white-house-card-led-topuria-gaethje-pereira-gane|title=UFC's White House card to be led by Topuria-Gaethje, Pereira-Gane|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2026-03-07|access-date=2026-03-08|archive-date=March 8, 2026|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260308080528/https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/48138882/ufc-white-house-card-led-topuria-gaethje-pereira-gane|url-status=live}}\n\n== Logistics and planning ==\n[[File:UFC_Freedom_250_arena_at_the_White_House_Washington_DC_20260-6-08_13-36-45.jpg|thumb|The event's venue under construction at the [[White House South Lawn]]]]\n\nIn a September 2025 interview, White said the seating capacity would be under 5,000 due to security concerns, a significant reduction from Trump's earlier estimate of 20,000 to 25,000 attendees. In a February 2026 interview, [[TKO Group Holdings]] CEO [[Ari Emanuel]] stated that there would be 3,000 to 4,000 people in attendance.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/47851578/ufc-white-house-event-6-7-fights-tko-says|title=UFC White House event will have 6-7 fights, TKO says|work=espn.com|author=Andreas Hale|date=2026-02-06|access-date=2026-02-06|archive-date=February 10, 2026|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260210062352/https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/47851578/ufc-white-house-event-6-7-fights-tko-says|url-status=live}} White revealed in May that the capacity was expected to be 4,300.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/05/04/dana-white-ufc-freedom-250-at-white-house-tickets/89930789007/|title=Dana White reveals more about UFC Freedom 250 at White House tickets|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Josh Peter|date=2026-05-04|access-date=2026-05-05|archive-date=May 18, 2026|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260518021718/https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/05/04/dana-white-ufc-freedom-250-at-white-house-tickets/89930789007/|url-status=live}} He also mentioned plans to install large screens at [[The Ellipse]] capable of hosting up to 85,000 spectators for a public viewing experience.{{Cite web|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/ufc-ceo-dana-white-capacity-will-be-limited-for-event-at-white-house-but-has-plans-for-fans-to-attend-nearby/|title=UFC CEO Dana White capacity will be limited for event at White House, but has plans for fans to attend nearby|publisher=cbssports.com|author=Shakiel Mahjouri|date=2025-09-11|access-date=2025-09-11|archive-date=September 12, 2025|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250912004215/https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/ufc-ceo-dana-white-capacity-will-be-limited-for-event-at-white-house-but-has-plans-for-fans-to-attend-nearby/|url-status=live}} White later announced that the 85,000 tickets for the viewing area would be available for free.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/48196436/dana-white-ufc-issue-85000-free-tickets-white-house-card|title=Dana White: UFC to issue 85,000 free tickets for White House card|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2026-03-13|access-date=2026-03-13|archive-date=March 13, 2026|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260313203522/https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/48196436/dana-white-ufc-issue-85000-free-tickets-white-house-card|url-status=live}} The venue also held a fan fest the day before, headlined by the [[Zac Brown Band]], and featured the ceremonial weigh-ins, meet and greets, on-stage entertainment, interactive experiences, and appearances from UFC athletes and other celebrities.{{Cite web|url=https://www.hollywoodreporter.com/music/music-news/zac-brown-band-headline-ufc-white-house-event-concert-1236561510/|title=Zac Brown Band Will Headline UFC Event on D.C.'s Ellipse Ahead of White House Bout (Exclusive)|publisher=hollywoodreporter.com|author=Alex Weprin|date=2026-04-11|access-date=2026-04-12}}\n\nTo prepare for the event, [[Stageco]] constructed a 92-foot tall arch called \"the Claw\" around an eight-sided fighting pit known as the \"Octagon\".{{Cite web |title=Everything to know about the UFC White House centerpiece: The claw |url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/48946908/ufc-freedom-250-white-house-claw |access-date=2026-07-11 |website=www.espn.com |date=June 10, 2026 }} Further reporting described the South Lawn audience as an invite-only crowd. ''[[Time (magazine)|Time]]'' reported that at least 1,200 of the approximately 4,300 seats would be allocated to active military members, while the remaining seats would be divided among the White House, TKO Group Holdings and the UFC. Tickets to the Ellipse events remained free but required registration, while South Lawn attendees were expected to present identification.{{Cite web|url=https://time.com/article/2026/05/26/dana-white-ufc-white-house-fight-interview/|title=Inside Dana White's Plan for a White House UFC Fight|publisher=time.com|author=Sean Gregory|date=2026-05-26|access-date=2026-05-30}} In a separate interview with [[TMZ Sports]], White said Trump had 1,000 tickets, White had 200, Emanuel had 200, and that the remaining seats would go to the military.{{Cite web|url=https://www.tmz.com/2026/05/18/white-house-fight-ticket-breakdown/|title=Dana White Reveals How Many White House Tickets He & Trump Have For Freedom 250|publisher=tmz.com|author=Staff|date=2026-05-18|access-date=2026-05-30}}\n\nOn May 29, ''[[The Washington Post]]'' reported that the [[United States Department of Defense|Department of Defense]] had solicited military personnel to attend the event, with internal messages directing commands to identify junior enlisted personnel and junior officers. According to the report, personnel selected for tickets were required to pay their own travel costs, wear short-sleeve dress uniforms, and meet current waist-to-height ratio and physical fitness standards. One message reviewed by the newspaper also said tickets should be distributed to \"genuine UFC fans\" rather than high-ranking distinguished visitors.{{Cite news|url=https://www.washingtonpost.com/national-security/2026/05/29/pentagon-recruiting-troops-watch-white-house-ufc-fights-memos-show/|title=Pentagon recruiting troops to watch White House UFC fights, memos show|work=washingtonpost.com|author=Dan Lamothe and Alex Horton|date=2026-05-29|access-date=2026-05-30|archive-date=May 31, 2026|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260531000431/https://www.washingtonpost.com/national-security/2026/05/29/pentagon-recruiting-troops-watch-white-house-ufc-fights-memos-show/|url-status=live}}\n\n[[Department of Homeland Security]] officials told [[TMZ]] that the event would feature Level 1 [[Special Event Assessment Rating]] (SEAR) security measures for the card happening on the South Lawn. The fan fest event, scheduled for June 13, would also feature SEAR 1 level security. Typically events that feature that level of security have multiple agencies monitoring and assistance could include explosive detection canine teams, cyber risk assessments, venue screening, field intelligence teams, air security and tactical operations support, including a no-drone zone, ground security as well as screening and surveillance along with security checkpoints.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/483840/ufc-white-house-event-to-feature-super-bowl-level-security-from-federal-agencies|title=UFC White House event to feature Super Bowl level security from Federal agencies|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2026-04-20|access-date=2026-04-21}}\n\nDuring a fight week media day on June 10, UFC [[Chief Content Officer]] Craig Borsari spoke to reporters about possible issues with the weather and other logistical complications. Two obvious concerns raised by media, fighters, and fans alike were how the UFC planned to deal with the summer sun and heat, and the possibility of rain—or worse, a thunderstorm. He stated that \"part of the reason why we picked an 8{{Nbsp}}p.m. start time was so that we could get some of the intense direct sunlight off of the canvas\" and revealed they have a custom cover for the canvas, as well as a {{convert|100|ft|m|adj=on}} canopy or tarp “that also provides a lot of shade and reduces the heat quite a bit.” He also stated that light to moderate rain with no lightning will not stop the event and in the worst-case weather scenario, they have a contingency planning.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/493341/ufc-white-house-producer-details-planning-for-outdoor-elements-bad-weather-contingencies|title=UFC White House producer details planning for outdoor elements, bad weather contingencies|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2026-06-10|access-date=2026-06-10}}\n\n===Budget and production===\n[[File:P20260506MR-0725 President Donald J. Trump speaks with members of the media alongside UFC fighters in the Oval Office.jpg|thumb|right|A custom belt designed exclusively for this event was revealed on May 6 and was awarded to both title fight winners.{{Cite web|url=https://bleacherreport.com/articles/25425615-new-ufc-white-house-custom-title-belt-revealed-photos-freedom-250-fight-card|title=New UFC White House Custom Title Belt Revealed in Photos for Freedom 250 Fight Card|publisher=bleacherreport.com|author=Andrew Peters|date=2026-05-06|access-date=2026-05-06}}]]\n\nThe promotion is expected to cover the full cost of the event, including roughly $700,000 to restore the South Lawn after use, and no public tickets will be made available.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/latest-news/406741/ufc-will-spend-700000-replacing-white-house-lawn|title=UFC will spend $700,000 replacing White House lawn|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Jed In January 2026, it was reported that no taxpayer funding is being sought, and the overall expense of the event is projected to exceed the $21 million budget required to stage [[UFC 306]]'s [[Sphere (venue)|Sphere]] show in September 2024.{{Cite February 2026, it was estimated the cost rose to $60 million.{{Cite news|url=https://puck.news/trumps-ufc-math-problem-rob-reiners-maga-partner/|title=Trump's UFC math problem and Rob Reiner's MAGA partner|publisher=puck.news|author=Kim Masters|date=2026-02-24|access-date=2026-02-25}} TKO president [[Mark Shapiro (media executive)|Mark Shapiro]] stated that the event is not expected to turn a profit, with the goal being to recoup approximately half of the total cost through sponsors and new partnerships.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/02/25/tko-mark-shapiro-ufc-white-house-financial-cost-no-profit/88869639007/|title=TKO exec firmly states UFC will not profit from White House event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matthew Wells|date=2026-02-26|access-date=2026-02-26|archive-date=February 26, 2026|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260226193726/https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/02/25/tko-mark-shapiro-ufc-white-house-financial-cost-no-profit/88869639007/|url-status=live}} In a June 2026 interview, White contended the cost would be worth it to propel MMA further into the mainstream, stating: \"This is a huge brand play.\"{{Cite news|url=https://www.forbes.com/sites/mattcraig/2026/06/05/inside-dana-whites-60-million-plan-to-stage-ufc-freedom-250-at-the-white-house/|title=Inside Dana White's $60 Million Plan To Stage UFC Freedom 250 At The White House|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matt Craig|date=2026-06-05|access-date=2026-06-10|archive-date=June 6, 2026|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260606131121/https://www.forbes.com/sites/mattcraig/2026/06/05/inside-dana-whites-60-million-plan-to-stage-ufc-freedom-250-at-the-white-house/|url-status=live}}\n\nAlthough no public tickets were made available, combat sports journalist [[Ariel Helwani]] reported in May 2026 that the UFC had circulated a \"partner investment\" deck offering special access at a reported $1.5 million price point. The reported package included access to a partner welcome reception, reserved press-conference seating, ceremonial weigh-ins, the [[Zac Brown Band]] concert, floor seats for [[UFC 329]], and [[WWE]] event integration and ring signage. [[BBC Sport]] reported that UFC sources confirmed the existence of special-guest packages but did not confirm the price.{{Cite web|url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/mixed-martial-arts/articles/cglp08jglpwo|title=UFC Freedom 250: Why White House event access could cost special guests $1.5m|work=BBC Sport|author=Paul Battison|date=2026-05-07|access-date=2026-05-30|archive-date=May 11, 2026|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260511225239/https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/mixed-martial-arts/articles/cglp08jglpwo|url-status=live}} [[NBC News]] also reported that sponsorship packages including ringside seats had been selling for $1 million or more, while the tickets themselves were free.{{cite web|url=https://www.nbcnews.com/politics/donald-trump/cage-match-tickets-trump-ufc-fight-white-house-rcna342904|title=The cage match is on for tickets to Trump's UFC fight at the White House|publisher=nbcnews.com|author=Jonathan Allen, Peter Nicholas, Matt Dixon, Henry J. Gomez and Allan Smith|date=2026-05-11|access-date=2026-06-08|archive-date=June 8, 2026|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260608024945/https://www.nbcnews.com/politics/donald-trump/cage-match-tickets-trump-ufc-fight-white-house-rcna342904|url-status=live}}\n\n===Regulation===\n[[Marc Ratner]], the UFC Vice President of Regulatory Affairs, revealed that because the White House grounds are federal property, the District of Columbia Combat Sports Commission would not regulate the event. The UFC was then expected to self‑regulate, as the [[District of Columbia Department of Parks and Recreation]] is responsible for oversight of such events within the district.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/01/30/marc-ratner-ufc-may-self-regulate-white-house-federal-land/88434643007|title=Marc Ratner: UFC may self-regulate White House card due to 'federal land'|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn, George Garcia and Brian Garcia|date=2026-01-30|access-date=2026-01-30|archive-date=January 31, 2026|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260131060317/https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/01/30/marc-ratner-ufc-may-self-regulate-white-house-federal-land/88434643007/|url-status=live}}\n\nOn March 16, the District of Columbia Combat Sports Commission announced that outcomes of the fights in this event could not be recognized on the athletes’ official records as the promotion was not going to pay a $100 permit to hold the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/DC-Commission-UFC-White-House-card-requires-permit-to-be-sanctioned-200477|title=D.C. Commission: UFC White House card requires permit to be sanctioned|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Sayan Nag|date=2026-03-16|access-date=2026-03-19}} In turn, the UFC announced that the [[Association of Boxing Commissions]] (ABC), which oversees boxing and MMA in the U.S., will be on hand to \"serve as an independent third party to advise on the regulatory operations\" of the event. \"As the event is being held on federal property, there is no requirement for the UFC to select a state athletic commission to oversee the event,\" ABC president Timothy Shipman said in a release. \"The UFC expresses its commitment to ensuring that this event is among the most thoroughly regulated in the history of the sport and has requested that the ABC serve as an independent third party to assist in assembling the most qualified group of judges, referees and inspectors in the world.\"{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/03/19/ufc-white-house-fights-will-be-sanctioned-regulated-by-association-boxing-comissions-abc-mma/89224465007/|title=UFC White House fights will be sanctioned, regulated by ABC|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2026-03-19|access-date=2026-03-19|archive-date=March 20, 2026|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260320062000/https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/03/19/ufc-white-house-fights-will-be-sanctioned-regulated-by-association-boxing-comissions-abc-mma/89224465007/|url-status=live}} \n\n===Fight card planning===\n====Scheduled fights====\n[[File:Alex Pereira UFC 300.png|200px|thumb|right|If victorious, [[Alex Pereira]] would have been the first fighter to win a UFC title in three different divisions.]]\n\n[[File:UFC Freedom 250, the mixed martial arts event produced by the Ultimate Fighting Championship, Sunday, June 14, 2026, in the Grand Foyer of the White House - and fighter availability====\nDana White stated during the post-fight press conference for [[UFC 326]] in March 2026 that, although there had been discussions between the promotion and [[Jon Jones]], a former two-time [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship|UFC Light Heavyweight]] and one-time [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|Heavyweight Champion]], he was never considered as a potential headliner for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.cbssports.com/ufc/news/jon-jones-ufc-release-dana-white-health-white-house/|title=Jon Jones requests UFC release, disputing Dana White's stance on health, White comments, stating that he had been in negotiations with the UFC in the days leading up to the announcement.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/475703/dana-white-jon-jones-was-never-ever-ever-going-to-headline-ufc-white-house-card-very-fair-to-consider-him-retired|title=Dana White: Jon Jones was 'never, He also publicly requested his release from the promotion.{{Cite web|url=https://frontofficesports.com/jon-jones-ufc-lowballed-him-on-white-house-card-seeks-release/|title=Jon Jones: UFC 'Lowballed' Him on White House Card, Seeks Release|publisher=frontofficesports.com|author=Griffin Senyek|date=2026-03-10|access-date=2026-03-11}}\n\n[[File:P20260506MR-1514 President Donald J. He further stated that [[Conor McGregor]] was not in serious talks to appear at the event and was instead expected to return during International Fight Week in July.\n\nIn a June 2026 interview, White stated that had current heavyweight champion Aspinall not been injured, he would have been defending his title against Pereira.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/ufc/story/_/id/49000739/dana-white-tom-aspinall-100-fighting-alex-pereira-ufc-white-house-freedom-250|title=Dana White: Fit Tom Aspinall would '100%' be fighting Alex Topuria and his manager stated that he had been offered current [[UFC Welterweight Championship|UFC Welterweight Champion]] (also former lightweight champion) [[Islam Makhachev]] as a potential opponent before the bout with Gaethje was finalized.{{Cite web|url=https://www.lowkickmma.com/ilia-topuria-ducking-islam-makhachev-accusation/|title=Ilia Topuria accuses Islam Makhachev of ducking UFC his preferred opponent and expressed interest in moving up to challenge for the welterweight title in an attempt to become the first three-division champion in UFC history, but Makhachev informed UFC officials that an injury prevented him from competing.{{Cite web|url=https://www.cbssports.com/ufc/news/ilia-topuria-islam-makhachev-white-house-card/|title=Ilia Topuria accuses Islam Makhachev of ducking him for White House fight, Makhachev responds|publisher=cbssports.com|author=Brian Campbell|date=2026-03-10|access-date=2026-03-11}} In contrast to Topuria's claim that the bout fell through due to an injury on Makhachev's side, Makhachev stated that the negotiations broke down because Topuria and his team declined the UFC's offer after requesting $20 million. According to Makhachev, that demand ultimately prevented the fight from being finalized.{{Cite web|url=https://timesofindia.indiatimes.com/sports/international-sports/islam-makhachev-reveals-fresh-twist-in-ilia-topuria-saga-as-reported-20-million-demand-and-failed-ufc-fight-return-to-spotlight/articleshow/131709328.cms|title=Islam Makhachev reveals fresh twist in Ilia Topuria saga as reported $20 million demand and failed UFC fight return to spotlight|publisher=indiatimes.com|author=Global Sports Desk|date=2026-06-13|access-date=2026-06-15}}\n\nAccording to Helwani, the UFC's original planning for the event included a potential [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]] bout between reigning champion [[Kayla Harrison]], who is a [[Judo at the 2012 Summer Olympics – Women's 78 kg|2012]] and [[Judo at the 2016 Summer Olympics – Women's 78 kg|2016 Olympic gold medalist in judo]] and a two-time [[Professional Fighters League|PFL]] women's lightweight tournament champion, and former two-time champion [[Amanda Nunes]], who also held the [[UFC Women's Featherweight Championship]]. However, Harrison was not ready to compete. He also reported that Pereira vs. Gane had initially been planned as the main event before the promotion asked Topuria whether he preferred to face Makhachev at welterweight or Gaethje at lightweight. Topuria reportedly chose Makhachev, but the bout was not made after Makhachev was unavailable due to a hand injury, leading to Gaethje receiving the title fight.{{Cite web|url=https://talksport.com/mma/4037799/ilia-topuria-islam-makhachev-ufc-white-house-super-fight/|title=Ilia Topuria picked Islam Makhachev for UFC White House – but injury prevented dream The fighters entered the Octagon from various locations inside the White House, including the [[Oval Office]] for both headliners, with their walkout music performed live by the [[United States Marine Band]].{{Cite web|url=https://nypost.com/2026/06/14/us-news/ufc-freedom-250-card-draws-thousands-to-dc-on-trumps-80th-birthday/|title=Wild 'UFC Freedom 250' card on White House South Lawn draws rave for upcoming fights and promotional videos between fights, this event played vignettes celebrating the 250th anniversary of the United States, much like the [[UFC 306]] event that took place inside [[Sphere (venue)|Sphere]] in [[Las Vegas]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/492247/ufc-plans-to-tell-the-story-of-america-in-between-fights-at-the-white-house|title=UFC plans to 'tell 2026|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260606115140/https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/492247/ufc-plans-to-tell-the-story-of-america-in-between-fights-at-the-white-house|url-status=live}}\n\n==Legal challenges==\nOn June 6, 2026, an emergency lawsuit was filed by the Public Integrity Project, an anti-corruption [[watchdog group]], on behalf of two plaintiffs, which aimed to block the event from occurring, describing it as a \"deeply corrupt ... private, for-profit sports event\".{{cite news|url=https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/jun/08/trump-ufc-white-house-birthday-lawsuit|title=Lawsuit aims to block UFC event at White House on Trump's birthday|publisher=theguardian.com|author=Joseph Gedeon|date=2026-06-08|access-date=2026-06-08}} The lawsuit challenged the transformation of the White House grounds without congressional approval and the financial benefits of the event for the UFC, Dana White, Donald Trump, and their allies. A Trump administration official responded to the suit, calling it \"obstructionist\" and stating that the fight card was no different than other events hosted at the White House.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/06/07/lawsuit-seeks-to-shut-down-ufc-white-house-event/90447637007/|title=Lawsuit seeks to shut down UFC White House event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2026-06-07|access-date=2026-06-07}}{{cite web|url=https://www.nbcnews.com/politics/white-house/federal-lawsuit-aims-stop-ufc-event-white-house-south-lawn-rcna348910|title=Federal lawsuit aims to stop UFC event on the White House South Lawn|publisher=nbcnews.com|author=Raquel Coronell Uribe|date=2026-06-07|access-date=2026-06-08|archive-date=June 8, 2026|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260608041308/https://www.nbcnews.com/politics/white-house/federal-lawsuit-aims-stop-ufc-event-white-house-south-lawn-rcna348910|url-status=live}} The lawsuit also highlighted that Trump purchased between $15,001 and $50,000 worth of stock in TKO Group Holdings, the UFC's parent company, in March 2026. A [[Citizens for Responsibility and Ethics in Washington]] spokesperson called it \"one of the worst conflicts of interest you could imagine\".{{cite news|author=Adam Edelman|date=2026-05-29|title=Trump's Stock Purchases Expose Massive Grift in UFC White House Fight|url=https://www.huffingtonpost.co.uk/entry/trump-ufc-stock-white-house-fight_n_6a19b50be4b0dade602f5c5c|publisher=huffingtonpost.co.uk|access-date=2026-06-08|archive-date=June 8, 2026|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260608175453/https://www.huffingtonpost.co.uk/entry/trump-ufc-stock-white-house-fight_n_6a19b50be4b0dade602f5c5c|url-status=live}} White later said that they were already expecting a lawsuit, but they \"thought it would be sooner\".{{cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/06/09/dana-white-video-comment-lawsuit-to-stop-ufc-freedom-250-was-expected-white-house/90483288007/|title=Dana White: Lawsuit to stop UFC Freedom 250 was expected|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matt Erickson and Mike Bohn|date=2026-06-09|access-date=2026-06-09|archive-date=June 10, 2026|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260610014804/https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/06/09/dana-white-video-comment-lawsuit-to-stop-ufc-freedom-250-was-expected-white-house/90483288007/|url-status=live}} On June 10, attorneys representing the [[National Park Service]] and [[U.S. Department of the Interior]] filed a memorandum in opposition to the plaintiffs’ emergency application for a temporary restraining order.{{cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/06/10/ufc-white-house-justice-department-trump-response-lawsuit-stop-event-illegal-dana-white/90490204007/|title=Government fires back against lawsuit to stop UFC White House event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Josh Peter|date=2026-06-10|access-date=2026-06-10}} On June 12, Judge [[Amit Mehta]] of the [[U.S. District Court for the District of Columbia]] refused to issue a preliminary injunction, allowing the event to proceed as planned.{{cite planned|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Steve Gardner and Josh Peter|date=2026-06-12|access-date=2026-06-13}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Fight card Championship|access-date=2026-03-07|archive-date=March 9, 2026|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260309202134/https://www.ufc.com/event/ufc-freedom-250|url-status=live}}\n\n==Bonus awards==\nIn April 2026, the UFC announced that [[Crypto.com]] would fund a US$1 million [[cryptocurrency]] bonus for the event, intended to reward the top performances on the card.{{Cite web|url=https://bloodyelbow.com/2026/04/11/ufc-white-house-fighters-could-win-1-million-bonus-as-sponsor-stumps-up-for-historic-event/|title=UFC White House fighters could win $1 million bonus as sponsor stumps up for historic event|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Donagh Corby|date=2026-04-11|access-date=2026-04-12|archive-date=April 12, 2026|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260412160849/https://bloodyelbow.com/2026/04/11/ufc-white-house-fighters-could-win-1-million-bonus-as-sponsor-stumps-up-for-historic-event/|url-status=live}} The special $1 million cryptocurrency prize pool created for the event was split evenly between Justin Gaethje and Ilia Topuria.{{Cite web|url=https://www.telecomasia.net/news/mma/topuria-and-gaethje-to-split-usd1-million-crypto-bonus/|title=Topuria and Gaethje to split $1 million crypto bonus|publisher=telecomasia.net|author=Marina Magomedova|date=2026-06-16|access-date=2026-06-16}}\n\nOn June 12, 2026, Dana White announced that the fight night awards for this event would receive a major increase as [[World Liberty Financial]] (a venture of the [[Trump family]] and the family of [[Steve Witkoff]], Trump’s friend and special envoy to the Middle East) joined as a new sponsor and added $250,000 to the bonus pool. In addition to the standard UFC amounts, \"Performance of the Night\" recipients earned $100,000 from the UFC, $125,000 in [[World Liberty Financial#USD1|USD1]] sponsored by World Liberty Financial, and $200,000 in CRO sponsored by Crypto.com, for a total of $425,000 each. \"Fight of the Night\" winners received $100,000 from the UFC along with $300,000 in CRO from Crypto.com, totaling $400,000 per athlete. The additional $25,000 UFC finish bonuses for knockouts or submissions not selected among the main post‑fight awards also remained in effect.{{Cite web|url=https://heavy.com/sports/ufc/dana-white-huge-bonuses-white-house/|title=Dana White announces monster bonuses for UFC White House|publisher=heavy.com|author=Adam Martin|date=2026-06-12|access-date=2026-06-12}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/ufc-bonuses-and-awards/451101/justin-gaethje-ufc-white-house-bonuses-staggering-825000-payout|title=Cha-ching! Justin Gaethje leads monster UFC White House bonuses with staggering $825,000 payout|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Jesse Holland|date=2026-06-15|access-date=2026-06-16}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.forbes.com/sites/brianmazique/2026/06/15/ufc-freedom-250-results-bonus-winners-highlights-and-reaction/|title=UFC Freedom 250 results: Bonus winners, highlights and reaction|work=forbes.com|author=Brian Mazique|date=2026-06-15|access-date=2026-06-15}}\n*'''Fight of the Night: Justin Gaethje vs. Ilia Topuria'''\n*'''Performance of the Night: Justin Gaethje''' and '''Ciryl Gane'''\n\n==Aftermath==\nThis event marked the [[List of UFC records#Highest percentage of knockouts - In a single event|only UFC event to end with all fights ending by knockout]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.sportsbusinessjournal.com/Articles/2026/06/15/ufc-pulls-off-first-white-house-sports-event-with-freedom-250/|title=UFC pulls off first White House sports event with Freedom 250|publisher=sportsbusinessjournal.com|author=Adam Stern|date=2026-06-15|access-date=2026-06-16}} It also tied events [[UFC Fight Night: Rockhold vs. Bisping]] and [[UFC on ESPN: Santos vs. Hill]] for having [[List of UFC records#Highest percentage of finishes - In a single event|all fights end by finish at a modern-era UFC event]], which had 11 and 10 fights respectively.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2022/8/7/23295151/ufc-vegas-59-bonus-winners-highlights-results-espn-mma-santos-hill-usman-neal-battle|title=UFC Vegas 59 bonuses: Usman, Neal lead the way for $50K winners|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Dan Hiergesell|date=2022-08-07|access-date=2022-08-07|archive-date=December 28, 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20221228010622/https://www.mmamania.com/2022/8/7/23295151/ufc-vegas-59-bonus-winners-highlights-results-espn-mma-santos-hill-usman-neal-battle|url-status=live}}\n\nIn a post-fight interview with [[Joe Rogan]], fighter [[Josh Hokit]] repeated previously stated false claims that former [[First Lady]] [[Michelle Obama]] is [[Transvestigation|actually a man]]. His comments drew outrage throughout social media. Dana White told ''Time'' he is \"completely against saying nasty and false things about people's families.\" However, no sanction was announced.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/06/15/ufc-white-house-josh-hokit-michelle-obama-comment/90554713007/|title=Josh Hokit's Michelle Obama comment at UFC White House sparks outrage|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Josh Peter|date=2026-06-15|access-date=2026-06-15}}\n\n===Attempted terrorist attack===\nAfter the event, the [[FBI]] claimed they disrupted an attempted terrorist attack targeting attendees of the event. According to investigators, the plan involved using [[One-way attack drone|explosive-laden drones]] to strike buildings near the event, triggering an evacuation. Attendees would then be directed toward an area where snipers were positioned, followed by a planned attack on the White House gates. The FBI reportedly discovered the threat on June 10, with suspects allegedly conducting final preparations in [[Fredericksburg, Virginia]]. Numerous suspects were later taken into custody.{{Cite news|last1=Lebowitz|first1=Megan|last2=Grumbach|first2=Gary|last3=Kosnar|first3=Michael|last4=Winter|first4=Tom|date=June 26, 2026|title=FBI arrests 5 in alleged plot to attack White House UFC event with explosives-laden drones and guns|work=NBC News|url=https://www.nbcnews.com/politics/trump-administration/fbi-foils-alleged-plot-attack-white-house-ufc-event-patel-says-rcna350248|access-date=June 26, 2026}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/494858/report-fbi-stopped-violent-terrorist-plot-aimed-at-ufc-white-house|title=Report: FBI stopped violent terrorist plot aimed at UFC White House, Donald Trump responds|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2026-06-15|access-date=2026-06-15}}\n\nOn July 9, 2026, the [[United States Department of Justice|U.S. Department of Justice]] announced that a federal grand jury has indicted eight men over an alleged plot to attack the event.{{cite web | last1=Singh | first1=Kanishka | title=Federal grand jury indicts 8 men over alleged plot to attack White House UFC event, DOJ says | work=Reuters | date=July 9, 2026 | url=https://www.reuters.com/legal/government/federal-grand-jury-indicts-8-men-over-alleged-plot-attack-white-house-ufc-event-2026-07-09/ }}\n\n==Reception==\n''[[The New York Times]]'' described it as a \"mass-media spectacle unlike any in the history of the presidency\", highlighting its use of the White House for \"violent cage fighting\" and eruptions of \"casual cruelty\" as \"astonishing\". The ''[[Associated Press]]'' described the event as \"[[bread and circuses]]\" and interviewed historians who compared it to gladiatorial games held in [[Imperial Rome]].{{cite news|author=Will Weissert|date=2026-06-15|title=Trump celebrates 80th birthday with an Iran deal and UFC cage fights at the White House|url=https://apnews.com/article/trump-80th-birthday-ufc-biden-e14d1bbccc1cbaaad42fd541b1fe833d|publisher=apnews.com|access-date=2026-06-15}} Conversely, [[Michael Caputo]] praised the event in ''[[The American Conservative]]'', dismissing critics as elitist \"[[snob]]s\".{{cite news|url=https://www.theamericanconservative.com/what-the-snobs-miss-about-ufc-freedom-250/|title=What the Snobs Miss About UFC Freedom 250|publisher=theamericanconservative.com|author=Michael Caputo|date=2026-06-19|access-date=2026-06-19}} Polling conducted by [[Reuters]] and [[Ipsos]] found that only 16% of Americans found the event appropriate to hold on the White House lawn, with only a third of Republicans approving of the plan.{{cite web |last1=Lange |first1=Jason |title=Few Americans back Trump's White House cage match plan, Reuters/Ipsos poll finds |url=https://www.reuters.com/world/us/few-americans-back-trumps-white-house-cage-match-plan-reutersipsos-poll-finds-2026-06-11/ |publisher=Reuters |access-date=8 July 2026 |date=June 11, 2026}}\n===Viewership===\nAccording to Dana White, the card brought in high numbers of viewers with reportedly 200,000 fans attending the two-day event on the Ellipse, and that viewership numbers on Paramount+ were \"monstrous\".{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/49070230/ufc-white-house-success-never-again-dana-white-says|title=UFC at White House a success, but never again, Dana White says|work=espn.com|author=Michael Rothstein|date=2026-06-15|access-date=2026-06-15}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/ufc-white-house-2026-fight-card-start-time-full-results-dana-white-conor-mcgregor-cbs-mma/451009/dana-white-reveals-ufc-white-house-event-delivered-did-monstrous-numbers-on-paramount|title=Dana White reveals UFC White House event 'delivered,' did 'monstrous' ratings on Paramount+|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Ryan Harkness|date=2026-06-15|access-date=2026-06-15}} It was broadcast exclusively on [[Paramount+]] in the U.S. and Latin America, with a reported average of 8.2 million viewers and at least 17 million unique viewers making it the most-watched exclusive live event on Paramount+ to-date.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/06/18/ufc-white-house-draws-big-viewership-did-it-meet-expectations/90614524007/|title=UFC White House event draws 17 million total viewers on Paramount+|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matthew Wells|date=2026-06-18|access-date=2026-06-18}} \n\nThe UFC stated that updated international metrics increased total viewership for the event to an estimated 34 million, setting the domestic record for the most‑watched event in the promotion's history.{{Cite web|url=https://www.nytimes.com/athletic/7396380/2026/06/26/ufc-freedom-250-white-house-viewership-update/|title=UFC puts total Freedom 250 viewers at 34 million based on new international data|author=Matt Moret|date=2026-06-26|publisher=theathletic.com|access-date=2026-06-27}}\n\n== See also ==\n{{Commons category}}\n{{Portal|Martial Arts}}\n*[[2026 in UFC]]\n*[[List of current UFC event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/03/12/ufc-macau-song-yadong-vs-deiveson-figueiredo-main-event-booked/89125077007/|title=Song Yadong lands UFC Macau main event fight, looks to bounce Arena|publisher=agbrief.com|author=Kelsey Wilhelm|date=2026-03-31|access-date=2026-03-31}}\n\nA featherweight bout between [[Road to UFC Season 3#Featherweight|Road to UFC Season 3]] featherweight web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Kangjie-Zhu-sees-change-of-opponent-at-UFC-Macau-201113|title=Kangjie Zhu sees change of opponent at UFC Macau|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Sayan Aguilar was scheduled for the event, but Aguilar withdrew due to an injury and was replaced by Luis Gurule, shifting the pairing to a bantamweight contest.{{Cite web|title=Rei Tsuruya gets new opponent for UFC Macau next week|url=https://heavy.com/sports/ufc/rei-tsuruya-new-opponent-ufc-macau/|date=2026-05-21|access-date=2026-05-22|publisher=heavy.com|author=Marcel Dorff}}\n\nA welterweight [[UFC 329|UFC 329: McGregor vs. Holloway 2]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Fiziev vs. Torres''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 280''''') was a mixed martial arts event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on June 27, 2026, at the [[National Gymnastics Arena]] in [[Baku]], Azerbaijan.{{Cite web|url=https://www.azernews.az/sports/257339.html|title=Baku to host UFC Fight Night until 2028 [PHOTOS]|publisher=azernews.az|author=Laman Ismayilova|date=2026-04-21|access-date=2026-05-05}}\n\n==Background==\nThis event marked the promotion's second visit to Baku and first since [[UFC on ABC: Hill vs. Rountree Jr.]] in June 2025. It was also the first event after the promotion signed a multi-year cooperation agreement with the Ministry of Youth and Sports and the Baku City Circuit (BCC) Operations Company that will see Fight Night events being held annually in the city until 2028.\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Rafael Fiziev]] and [[Manuel Torres (fighter)|Manuel Torres]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/05/05/rafael-fiziev-vs-manuel-torres-headlines-ufc-fight-night-280-in-baku/89942165007/|title=UFC Baku: Rafael Fiziev gets home headliner vs. Manuel Torres|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2026-05-05|access-date=2026-05-05}} The card was originally rumored to be headlined by a middleweight bout between [[Michał Oleksiejczuk]] and [[Abusupiyan Magomedov]], but it was later moved to a different spot on the main card for undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|title=Nieoficjalnie – Michał Oleksiejczuk vs Abus Magomedov walką wieczoru czerwcowej gali ufc w baku!|url=https://www.mmarocks.pl/ufc/nieoficjalnie-michal-oleksiejczuk-vs-abus-magomedov-walka-wieczoru-czerwcowej-gali-ufc-w-baku|date=2026-04-17|access-date=2026-05-09|publisher=mmarocks.pl|author=Jakub Madej}} {{in lang|pl}}\n\nFormer [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] challenger [[Marvin Vettori]] was expected to face [[Ismail Naurdiev]] in a middleweight bout.{{Cite web|title=Marvin Vettori vs Ismail Naurdiev a UFC Baku il 27 giugno|url=https://www.theshieldofsports.news/marvin-vettori-ismail-naurdiev-ufc-baku/|date=2026-03-26|access-date=2026-03-30|publisher=theshieldofsports.news|author=Francesco Auletta}} {{in lang|it}} However, Vettori pulled out on May 21 due to a broken rib. Therefore, the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/05/21/marvin-vettori-out-ufc-fight-night-280-baku-rib-injury/90192468007/|title=Rib injury forces Marvin Vettori out of UFC Fight Night 280|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2026-04-21|access-date=2026-05-21}}\n\nA welterweight bout between Andreas Gustafsson and Daniil Donchenko was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.si.com/fannation/mma/news/ufc-reveals-banger-main-event-ufc-fight-night-baku|title=UFC reveals banger main event for upcoming UFC Fight Night in Baku|publisher=si.com|author=Drew Beaupre|date=2026-05-05|access-date=2026-05-28}} However, Gustafsson withdrew after revealing he had been suffering from [[atrial fibrillation]] and recurring heart rushes, and was replaced by promotional newcomer Theodor Berggren.{{Cite web|url=https://bloodyelbow.com/2026/05/26/ufc-fighter-dana-white-broke-his-rule-to-sign-forced-to-suspend-career-due-to-heart-issues/|title=UFC fighter Dana White broke his rule to sign forced to Holm|date=2026-06-06|access-date=2026-06-07}} {{in lang|sv}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Rizvan Kuniev]] and [[Tyrell Fortune]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.si.com/fannation/mma/news/ufc-reveals-banger-main-event-ufc-fight-night-baku|title=UFC reveals banger main event for upcoming UFC Fight Night in Baku|publisher=si.com|author=Drew produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on June 20, 2026, at the [[UFC Apex|Meta Apex]] in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas Valley]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/03/24/manel-kape-vs-kyoji-horiguchi-booked-ufc-fight-night-headliner/89301339007/|title=Manel Kape vs. Kyoji Horiguchi to headline UFC Fight Night in June|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura and Mike Bohn|date=2026-03-24|access-date=2026-03-25}}\n\n==Background==\nA flyweight rematch between former [[Rizin Fighting Federation#Bantamweight Championship|RIZIN Bantamweight Champion]] [[Manel Kape]] and former [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[Kyoji Horiguchi]] (who is also a former [[Rizin Fighting Federation#Flyweight Championship|RIZIN Flyweight]] and two-time RIZIN Bantamweight Champion, as well as former [[List of Bellator champions#Bantamweight World Championship|Bellator Bantamweight World Champion]]) served as the event headliner. The pair first met in December 2017 at [[2017 in Rizin Fighting Federation#Rizin World Grand Prix 2017: final round|Rizin World Grand Prix 2017: Final Round]], where Horiguchi defeated Kape in the semi-finals via arm-triangle choke submission in the third round.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2017/12/31/rizin-world-grand-prix-2017-final-round-recap-results/|title=Rizin World Grand Prix 2017 Final Round recap and results|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2017-12-31|access-date=2026-03-25}}\n\nMitch Raposo and [[Allan Nascimento]] were expected to meet in a flyweight bout at [[UFC Fight Night: Burns vs. Malott]] in April.{{Cite news|url=https://clutchpoints.com/ufc/mitch-raposo-vs-allan-nascimento-added-to-growing-ufc-ottawa-card-on-april-18|title=Mitch Raposo vs. Allan Nascimento added to growing UFC Ottawa card on April 18|publisher=clutchpoints.com|author=Garrett Kerman|date=2026-02-14|access-date=2026-04-19}} However, Raposo was forced off the event due to illness, so the bout was re-scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/04/16/ufc-winnipeg-mitch-raposo-vs-allan-nascimento-off/89648584007/|title=UFC Winnipeg loses fight due to illness|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2026-04-16|access-date=2026-04-16}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Jhonata Diniz]] and José Luiz was scheduled for the event.{{Cite 329, Hall of Fame, more|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2026-04-05|access-date=2026-04-20}}\n\nA welterweight bout between former [[UFC Featherweight Championship|UFC Featherweight]] and [[UFC Lightweight Championship|Lightweight Champion]] [[Conor McGregor]] and fellow former featherweight champion [[Max Holloway]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|title=Conor Corby}} This was McGregor's first appearance since he broke his leg at [[UFC 264]] in July 2021, and it also marked Holloway's debut in the welterweight division. They previously fought in August 2013 at [[UFC Fight Night: Shogun vs. Sonnen]] in a featherweight bout early in their UFC careers, where McGregor won via unanimous decision.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2013/8/17/4632014/ufc-fight-night-26-results-conor-mcgregor-dominates-max-holloway|title=UFC Fight Night 26 results: Conor McGregor dominates Max Holloway|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2013-08-17|access-date=2026-05-17}} [[Maurício Ruffy]] served as backup and potential replacement for this fight.{{cite web|url=https://heavy.com/sports/ufc/mauricio-ruffy-backup-fighter-ufc-329/|title=Mauricio Ruffy announces he will be backup fighter at UFC 329|publisher=heavy.com|author=Adam Martin|date=2026-07-05|access-date=2026-07-05}}\n\nA welterweight bout between former [[UFC Welterweight Championship|UFC Welterweight Champion]] [[Leon Edwards]] and [[Daniel Rodriguez (fighter)|Daniel Rodriguez]] was reportedly linked to the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/ufc-news/436891/daniel-rodriguez-goes-from-mexican-jail-to-top-ten-opponent-at-ufc-329|title=Daniel Rodriguez goes from Mexican jail to top ten opponent at UFC 329|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Ryan Harkness|date=2026-04-15|access-date=2026-05-25}} However, Rodriguez was instead scheduled to face another opponent at another date.{{Cite web|url=https://heavy.com/sports/ufc/uros-medic-gets-against-daniel-rodriguez/|title=Uroš Medić gets main event at home against Daniel Rodriguez|publisher=heavy.com|author=Marcel Droff|date=2026-05-25|access-date=2026-05-25}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Farid Basharat]] and Ethyn Ewing was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/ufc-fight-announcements/448126/banger-king-green-vs-terrance-mckinney-added-to-ufc-329|title=Banger! The other finishes received $25,000 additional bonuses.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/ufc-329-mcgregor-vs-holloway-2/456336/bonuses-paddy-pimbletts-shocking-submission-earns-extra-100k-ufc-329|title=Bonuses! Paddy Pimblett's shocking submission earns extra $100k - UFC 329|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Andrew Richardson|date=2026-07-12|access-date=2026-07-12}}\n*'''Fight of the Night: Brandon Royval vs. Lone'er Kavanagh'''\n*'''Performance of the Night: Paddy Pimblett''' and '''King Green'''\n\n==Records set==\nThe event matched the UFC record for the [[List of UFC records#Most finishes - In a single event|most finishes in a single event]], tying [[UFC Fight Night: Rockhold vs. Bisping]], [[UFC 224]], [[UFC 281]] and [[UFC Fight Night: Imavov vs. Borralho]] with a total of 11 finishes.{{Cite web|url=https://bloodyelbow.com/2025/09/07/ufc-paris-tied-three-other-cards-for-a-record-after-legend-said-it-might-be-the-greatest-ufc-event-of-all-time/|title=UFC Paris tied three other cards for a record after legend said it might be 'the greatest UFC event of all time'|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Kyle Dimond|date=2025-09-07|access-date=2026-07-12}} The record was ultimately surpassed in August 2026 when [[UFC Fight Night: Medić vs. Rodriguez]] recorded twelve finishes.{{Cite web|url=https://heavy.com/sports/ufc/ufc-belgrade-bonuses-revealed-after-card/|title=UFC Belgrade bonuses revealed after record-setting card|publisher=heavy.com|author=Adam Martin|date=2026-08-01|access-date=2026-08-02}}\n\nThe event also set the record for the [[List of UFC records#Most first-round finishes – In a single event|most first-round finishes]] in a modern UFC event, with eight, a mark that was later surpassed one month later when [[UFC Fight Night: Medić vs. Rodriguez]] recorded nine.{{Cite web|url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/mixed-martial-arts/articles/ce97rzevgyjo|title=Medic marks UFC's Serbia debut with 30-second win|publisher=bbc.com|author=Bobbie Jackson|date=2026-08-02|access-date=2026-08-02}}\n\nThe event generated $26,430,566 in ticket receipts, which broke the record for the [[List_of_UFC_records#Biggest_gates|biggest gate in UFC history]] surpassing [[UFC 306]] in September 2024 (which previously held the record with a gate of $21,829,245).{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-event-gates-10-highest-ever|title=10 highest UFC event gates in promotion history|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2024-09-17|access-date=2026-07-12}}\n\n== See also ==\n{{Portal|Martial Arts}}\n*[[2026 in Championship]] that took place on August 15, 2026, at the [[Xfinity Mobile Arena]] in [[Philadelphia, Pennsylvania]], United States.{{Cite web |author=Staff |date=2026-04-14 |title=Philadelphia to host UFC 330 in August |url=https://www.sportsnet.ca/ufc/article/philadelphia-to-host-ufc-330-in-august/ |access-date=2026-04-15 |publisher=sportsnet.ca}} It was the twenty-sixth [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]] event of the year. The card consisted of twelve bouts and was streamed on [[Paramount+]].\n\nThe card's main event featured [[UFC Welterweight Championship|UFC Welterweight Champion]] [[Islam Makhachev]] defending his title against [[Ian Machado Garry]]. Makhachev defeated Garry by unanimous decision, surpassing [[Anderson Silva]]'s record for the [[List of UFC records#Most consecutive wins|longest winning streak in UFC history]]. The co-main event featured [[UFC Women's Strawweight Champion]] [[Mackenzie Dern]] defending her title against [[Gillian Robertson]].\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's fourth visit to Philadelphia, its first event since [[UFC on ESPN: Barboza vs. Gaethje]] in March 2019 and first numbered event since [[UFC 133]] in August 2011.\n\n=== Card changes ===\nA middleweight bout between [[Caio Borralho]] and [[Joe Pyfer]] was reportedly being targeted for the event. However, Borralho announced that he was unable to compete due to complications from a rib injury, and the matchup was abandoned.{{Cite web|url=https://bloodyelbow.com/2026/06/06/caio-borralho-reveals-hes-out-of-unannounced-ufc-330-matchup-due-to-significant-rib-injury/|title=Caio Borralho reveals he’s out of unannounced UFC 330 matchup due to significant rib injury|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Kyle Dimond|date=2026-06-06|access-date=2026-06-07}} [[Erin Blanchfield]] and [[Jasmine Jasudavicius]] were scheduled to meet in a women's flyweight bout at the event, but Blanchfield pulled out due to injury, so the bout was rescheduled for October at [[UFC Fight Night: Buckley vs. Malott]].{{Cite Thompson}}\n\n== Event ==\n\n=== Preliminary card ===\nIn the first fight of the event, [[Myktybek Orolbai]] faced [[Jeremiah Wells]]. The fight saw Orolbai have considerable success in the first two rounds, with Orolbai scoring takedowns on Wells and engaging in ground and pounds. However, during a striking exchange in the third round, Wells successfully put Orolbai in a ninja choke submission and put him unconscious.{{Cite web |last=Holt |first=Nick |date=2026-08-15 |title=UFC 330: Jeremiah Wells Submits Myktybek Orolbai in Thrilling Comeback, But Uncertain on Future |url=https://cagesidepress.com/2026/08/15/ufc-330-jeremiah-wells-submits-myktybek-orolbai-in-thrilling-comeback-but-uncertain-on-future/ |access-date=2026-08-23 |website=Cageside Press |language=en-US}}\n\n=== Main card ===\n\n==== Dern vs. Robertson ====\nA [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Mackenzie Dern]] and [[Gillian Robertson]] took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.lowkickmma.com/mackenzie-dern-vs-gillian-robertson-ufc-330/|title=Mackenzie Dern reportedly draws 25-fight strawweight veteran for first title defense at UFC 330 in Philadelphia|publisher=lowkickmma.com|author=Pranav Pandey|date=2026-05-20|access-date=2026-05-22}}\n\nThe fight saw Dern dominate Robertson for the first four rounds, until the visibly tired Dern lost the final round. Dern won the first round convincingly, controlling Robertson and nearly submitting her in the final 30 seconds as she tried to find a [[Rear naked choke|rear-naked choke]]. Dern continued to score takedowns against Robertson, winning over 10 minutes of control. Robertson saw success in the last round outstriking Dern, but was unable to capitalize enough for a finish and lost the fight by unanimous decision.{{Cite web |url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/49624575/dern-dominates-robertson-ground-retain-title-ufc-330 |access-date=2026-08-19 |website=[[ESPN]]|title=Dern dominates Robertson on ground to retain title at UFC 330|last=Okamoto|first=Brett}}\n\n==== Makhachev vs. Garry ====\nA [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] bout between current champion (also former [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]]) [[Islam Makhachev]] and [[Ian Machado Garry]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/495099/islam-makhachev-vs-ian-machado-garry-mackenzie-dern-vs-gillian-robertson-announced-for-ufc-330|title=Islam Makhachev vs. Ian Machado Garry, Mackenzie Dern vs. Gillian Robertson announced for UFC 330|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2026-06-18|access-date=2026-06-18}} [[Michael Morales (fighter)|Michael Morales]] served as backup and potential replacement for this fight should one of the contenders be injured.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/06/25/ufc-330-backup-fighter-michael-morales-makhachev-vs-machado-garry-title-fight/90692264007/|title=Michael Morales says he'll weigh in as backup for UFC 330 title fight|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Simon Samano|date=2026-06-25|access-date=2026-06-25}} Both fighters had weighed in successfully at exactly 170lb.{{Cite web |date=2026-08-14 |title=Official Weigh-In Results {{!}} UFC 330: Makhachev vs Machado Garry {{!}} UFC |url=https://www.ufc.com/news/official-weigh-results-ufc-330-makhachev-vs-machado-garry |url=https://www.instagram.com/p/DcKHT4pO-3a/ |access-date=2026-08-19 |website=Instagram}}\n\nThe prelude to the fight was marked by Garry refusing to trash-talk or denigrate Makhachev's accomplishments, with Garry instead praising Makhachev as the \"best fighter walking the planet right now until proven otherwise.\"{{Cite web |date=2026-08-12 |title=Ian Garry Explains Why He Refuses to Trash Talk Islam Makhachev Before UFC 330 - NY FIGHTS |url=https://nyfights.com/news/ian-garry-explains-why-he-refuses-to-trash-talk-islam-makhachev-before-ufc-330/ |access-date=2026-08-19 |language=en-US}} Garry framed himself as the as best opponent Makhachev ever faced, focusing on promoting his own fight intelligence.{{Cite web |last=Aulino |first=Giancarlo |date=2026-08-05 |title=Ian Machado Garry Makes Bold Islam Makhachev Claim Ahead of UFC 330 |url=https://heavy.com/sports/ufc/ian-machado-garry-islam-makhachev-claim/ |access-date=2026-08-19 |website=HEAVY |language=en-US}} When Makhachev was asked a question about the late [[Abdulmanap Nurmagomedov]], Garry interrupted that Nurmagomedov would be proud, prompting ire from Makhachev.{{Cite web |date=2026-08-14 |title=Makhachev tells Garry to ‘shut up’ as UFC star answers emotional question for him opponent, stating that he can outstrike him and promised to try and finish him in the early rounds.{{Cite web |last=Martin |first=Damon |date=2026-08-12 |title=Islam Makhachev scoffs at Ian Machado Garry being his toughest opponent to date at UFC 330: ‘It’s not going to be my biggest challenge’ |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/504552/islam-makhachev-scoffs-at-ian-machado-garry-being-his-toughest-opponent-to-date-at-ufc-330-its-not-going-to-be-my-biggest-challenge |access-date=2026-08-19 |website=MMA Fighting: UFC, Mixed Martial Arts (MMA) News, Results |language=en-US}}{{Cite web |last=Rashid |first=Tom |last2= |first2= |date=2026-08-03 |title=Makhachev Vows To Take More Risks Against Garry |url=https://www.yardbarker.com/mma/articles/makhachev_vows_to_take_more_risks_against_garry/s1_17349_44134462 |access-date=2026-08-19 |website=Yardbarker |language=en}} Although some similarities were raised between [[Conor McGregor]]'s fight with [[Khabib Nurmagomedov]] at [[UFC 229]], owing to both bouts being between a Dagestani champion and an Irish challenger, the lack of significant animosity between the fighters was contrasted.{{Cite web |last=Hannoun |first=Farah |title=Coach: Makhachev vs. Machado Garry has 'zero similarities to Conor vs. Khabib' |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/07/29/coach-makhachev-vs-machado-garry-not-similar-to-conor-vs-khabib/91088863007/ |access-date=2026-08-19 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\nThe fight began with Makhachev taking down Garry in his first attempt, controlling the round with the challenger trapped against the cage. The second round saw Makhachev outstrike Garry standing up, landing a head kick that knocked Garry down and causing Garry to scramble to survive the round. The third round saw Garry see a significant amount of success with his kicks and punches, bloodying Makhachev's face with punches during clinches. The fourth round saw Makhachev continue to struggle to control Garry, pushing him against the wall and grappling him but being unable to secure significant control or land strikes on him. The fifth round saw Makhachev succeed in regaining control, taking Garry down and going to the back position to pummel Garry's face with elbows and punches. Although the fight saw Garry land 29 significant strikes to Makhachev's 22, the significant amount of control Makhachev demonstrated convinced the judges of his victory, with two judges giving all rounds except 3 to Makhachev, and the remaining judge giving rounds 3 and 4 to Garry.{{Cite web |date=2026-08-16 |title=UFC 330: Islam Makhachev beats Ian Machado Garry to break UFC win record |url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/mixed-martial-arts/articles/c8dnn7d01p5o |access-date=2026-08-18 |website=BBC Sport |language=en-GB}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card The other finishes received $25,000 additional bonuses.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/08/16/ufc-330-bonuses-charles-johnson-scottish-twister-submission-jalin-turner-knockout-philadelphia-upset/91322855007/|title=UFC 330 bonuses: Ultra-rare Scottish twister leads $100K Philly winners|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matt Erickson|date=2026-08-16|access-date=2026-08-16}}\n*'''Fight of the Night:''' No bonus awarded.\n*'''Performance of the Night: Jalin Turner''', '''Dustin Stoltzfus''', '''Charles Johnson''', and '''Jeremiah Wells'''\n\n== Reception ==\nThe fight between Makhachev and Garry was deemed to be underwhelming by several pundits, who considered it to be a highly skilled, but less than entertaining affair. Jed Meshew of ''MMA Fighting'' described it as \"impressive,\" despite not being \"fun.\"{{Cite web |last=Meshew |first=Jed |date=2026-08-17 |title=UFC 330 Report: What do we make of Islam Makhachev’s win over Ian Machado Garry? |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/505335/ufc-330-report-what-do-we-make-of-islam-makhachevs-win-over-ian-machado-garry |access-date=2026-08-19 |website=MMA Fighting: UFC, Mixed Martial Arts (MMA) News, Results |language=en-US}} Kevin Kinkead of ''Crossing Board'' stated that the fight was \"not an instant classic,\" but acknowledged the talent of both fighters.{{Cite web |last=Kinkead |first=Kevin |date=2026-08-17 |title=10 Wrap-Up Thoughts on UFC 330 in South Philly |url=https://www.crossingbroad.com/news/trending/10-wrap-up-thoughts-on-ufc-330-in-south-philly/ |access-date=2026-08-19 |website=Crossing Broad |language=en-US}} Garry's repeated displays of respect earned the ire of [[Dana White]] and some observers, although he was defended by Chuck Mindenhall of ''Uncrowned'' for winning rounds off Makhachev.{{Cite web |last=read |first=Chuck MindenhallUncrowned·6 min |date=2026-08-16 |title=UFC 330: Love or hate Ian Machado Garry, he deserves respect after battling Islam Makhachev in Philadelphia |url=https://sports.yahoo.com/mma/article/ufc-330-love-or-hate-ian-machado-garry-he-deserves-respect-after-battling-islam-makhachev-in-philadelphia-083148936.html |access-date=2026-08-19 |website=Yahoo Sports |language=en-US}} [[Joe Rogan]] suggested following the fight that Makhachev could return to [[Lightweight (MMA)|Lightweight]], noting that he struggled against heavier opponents at 170 pounds.{{Cite web |date=2026-08-19 |title=Joe Rogan Shares Unique Possibility for Islam Makhachev After Record-Breaking UFC Win |url=https://www.si.com/fannation/mma/news/joe-rogan-shares-unique-possibility-for-islam-makhachev-after-record-breaking-ufc-win |access-date=2026-08-19 |website=MMA Knockout On SI |language=en-US}}\n\nWith his title victory, [[Islam Makhachev]] surpassed former [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] [[Anderson Silva]]'s record of 16 consecutive UFC wins. The record was held by Silva for nearly 14 years, as his 16-fight streak ran from June 2006 to October 2012. Makhachev's record-setting win streak began in September 2016.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/49625277/islam-makhachev-notches-17th-consecutive-victory-ufc-330|title=Islam Makhachev notches 17th consecutive victory at UFC 330|publisher=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2026-08-16|access-date=2026-08-16}} Commentators were divided over if this record being broken indicated if Makhachev was one of the greatest mixed martial artists of all time. [[Michael Chiesa]] argued that Makhachev had surpassed [[Georges St-Pierre|Georges St. Pierre]] by defending his title at multiple weight classes.{{Cite web |date=2026-08-18 |title=Islam Makhachev has surpassed Georges St-Pierre as the second best of all time says UFC commentator |url=https://bloodyelbow.com/2026/08/18/islam-makhachev-has-surpassed-georges-st-pierre-as-the-second-best-of-all-time-says-ufc-commentator/ |access-date=2026-08-19 |website=bloodyelbow.com |language=en-US}} ''[[Deadspin]]'' argued that Makhachev defending his title elevated him above other double champions, though noted that other records would require Makhachev to defend his title more times to be broken.{{Cite news |last=Puleo |first=Mark |date=2026-08-16 |title=Islam Makhachev breaks Anderson Silva’s UFC record with 17th straight win in title defense |url=https://www.nytimes.com/athletic/7516363/2026/08/16/islam-makhachev-ufc-330-ian-machado-garry/ |access-date=2026-08-17 |work=The New York Times |language=en-US |issn=0362-4331}}{{Cite web |title=Islam Makhachev Just Took His UFC Legacy to Another Level {{!}} Deadspin.com |url=https://deadspin.com/islam-makhachev-just-took-his-ufc-legacy-to-another-level/ |access-date=2026-08-17 contenders.{{Cite web |last=Chakraborty |first=Sourav |title=Islam Makhachev's UFC GOAT case gets pushback from former champs |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Islam-Makhachevs-UFC-GOAT-case-gets-pushback-from-former-champs-202431 |access-date=2026-08-19 |website=Sherdog |language=en}}\n\n== See also web|url=https://www.koco.com/article/ufc-oklahoma-okc-fight-night-report/71085179|title=Report: UFC event coming to Oklahoma for first time in nearly a decade|publisher=koco.com|author=Jonathan Greco|date=2026-04-21|access-date=2026-04-22}}\n\n==Background==\nThis event marked the promotion's third visit to Oklahoma City and first since [[UFC Fight Night: Chiesa vs. Lee]] in June 2017.\n\nA middleweight bout between former [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] (also former [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Welterweight championship|KSW Welterweight Champion]]) [[Dricus du Plessis]] and former [[UFC Welterweight Championship|UFC Welterweight Champion]] (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: American Top Team vs. Blackzilians]] welterweight winner) [[Kamaru Usman]] headlined the event.{{cite web|title=UFC returns to Oklahoma with Usman vs Du Plessis headlining July 18 card|url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/ufc-oklahoma-usman-vs-du-plessis/|author=Roberto Villa|publisher=fightbookmma.com|date=2026-05-20|access-date=2026-05-25}}\n\nA women's strawweight bout between [[Amanda Ribas]] and [[Fatima Kline]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/04/21/ufc-oklahoma-fatima-kline-vs-amanda-ribas/89720882007/|title=UFC books Fatima Kline vs. Amanda Ribas|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2026-04-21|access-date=2026-06-19}} However, Ribas withdrew due to dizziness and was replaced by [[Tabatha Ricci]].{{Cite web|url=https://heavy.com/sports/ufc/fatima-kline-opponent-ufc-oklahoma-city/|title=Fatima Kline gets new opponent for UFC Oklahoma City|publisher=heavy.com|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2026-06-17|access-date=2026-06-19}}\n\n[[Brad Tavares]] was expected to face [[Marc-André Barriault]] in a middleweight bout.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/ufc-fight-announcements/439916/fight-news-brad-tavares-collides-with-marc-andre-barriault-in-oklahoma-city-for-ufc-history|title=Fight news: Brad Tavares collides with so the bout was cancelled.{{Cite web|url=https://agentmma.com/news/85717-brad-tavares-withdraws-from-ufc-okc-bout-with-marc-andr-barriault|title=Brad Tavares withdraws from UFC OKC bout with
