Usman]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Medić vs. Rodriguez]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Ankalaev vs. Guskov''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 282''''') was a mixed martial arts event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on July 25, 2026, at the [[Etihad Arena]] in [[Abu Dhabi]], United Arab Emirates.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/05/18/ufc-abu-dhabi-fight-night-july-25-announcement-middle-east-mena-mma/90138869007/|title=UFC returns to Abu Dhabi for Fight Night event in July|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2026-05-18|access-date=2026-05-18}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's 23rd visit to Abu Dhabi and first since [[UFC 321]] in October 2025.\n\nA light heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship|UFC Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Magomed Ankalaev]] and former title challenger [[Khalil Rountree Jr.]] was scheduled to headline the event.{{Cite web|title=Former light heavyweight champion headlines UFC Abu Dhabi|url=https://heavy.com/sports/ufc/former-205-champ-headlines-abu-dhabi/|date=2026-05-22|access-date=2026-05-23|publisher=heavy.com|author=Adam Martin}} However, less than two weeks before the event, Rountree withdrew due to an injury and was replaced by [[Bogdan Guskov]], who was originally scheduled to compete one week later at [[UFC Fight Night: Medić vs. Rodriguez]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/499327/magomed-ankalaev-fights-bogdan-guskov-in-new-ufc-abu-dhabi-main-event-with-khalil-rountree-out|title=Magomed Ankalaev fights Bogdan Guskov in new UFC Abu Dhabi main event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2026-07-14|access-date=2026-07-14}} Former [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Light heavyweight championship|KSW]] and [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship|UFC Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Jan Błachowicz]], who was the opponent for Guskov the following week, was originally asked to step in on short notice but said he was not able to make the weight limit one week prior to his scheduled bout.\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Rizvan Kuniev]] and [[Tyrell Fortune]] took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.fightmag.com/ufc-abu-dhabi-2026-confirmed-for-july/|title=UFC Abu Dhabi 2026 confirmed for July|publisher=fightmag.com|author=Parviz Iskenderov|date=2026-05-18|access-date=2026-06-01}} The bout was originally scheduled to take place one month earlier at [[UFC Fight Night: Fiziev vs. Torres]] but was moved to this card for undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.si.com/fannation/mma/news/ufc-reveals-banger-main-event-ufc-fight-night-baku|title=UFC reveals banger main event for upcoming UFC Fight Night in Baku|publisher=si.com|author=Drew Beaupre|date=2026-05-05|access-date=2026-06-01}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between Louie Sutherland and Gökhan Saricam was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/06/01/ufc-abu-dhabi-main-event-magomed-ankalaev-vs-khalil-rountree/90217726007/|title=UFC Abu Dhabi main event features Magomed Ankalaev vs. Khalil Rountree|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2026-06-01|access-date=2026-06-24}} However, it was reported that Sutherland was instead scheduled to face another opponent at [[UFC Fight Night: Gamrot vs. Salkilld]] two weeks later.{{Cite web|url=https://heavy.com/sports/ufc/ranked-brazilian-women-battle-las-vegas/|title=Ranked Brazilian women battle in Las Vegas|publisher=heavy.com|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2026-06-23|access-date=2026-06-24}}\n\nPromotional newcomers Magomedrasul Gasanov and Abubakar Vagaev were scheduled to meet in a middleweight bout at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://fighttime.ru/news/item/42538-boj-gasanova-i-vagaeva-v-razrabotke-na-turnir-ufc-v-abu-dabi.html|title=Бой Гасанова и Вагаева в разработке на турнир UFC в Абу-Даби|publisher=fighttime.ru|author=Vladimir Artamonov|date=2026-05-25|access-date=2026-06-01}} {{in lang|ru}} However, Gasanov pulled out for unknown reasons and was replaced by Saygid Izagakhmaev, with the matchup subsequently being shifted to a welterweight contest.{{Cite web|date=2026-06-21|title=Соперником Абубакара Вагаева в UFC станет Сайгид Изагахмаев|url=https://fighttime.ru/news/item/42896-sopernikom-abubakara-vagaeva-v-ufc-stanet-sajgid-izagakhmaev.html|access-date=2026-07-07|publisher=fighttime.ru|author=Vladimir Artamonov}} {{in lang|ru}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between former [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] challenger [[Umar Nurmagomedov]] and [[David Martínez (fighter)|David Martínez]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/06/01/ufc-abu-dhabi-main-event-magomed-ankalaev-vs-khalil-rountree/90217726007/|title=UFC Abu Dhabi main event features Magomed Ankalaev vs. Khalil Rountree|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2026-06-01|access-date=2026-07-01}} However, Martínez withdrew from the bout due to an injury, so the bout was cancelled.{{Cite web|url=https://bloodyelbow.com/2026/06/10/umar-nurmagomedov-could-now-be-facing-a-top-5-contender-after-injury-cancels-david-martinez-fight/|title=Umar Nurmagomedov could now be facing a top-5 contender after injury cancels David Martinez fight|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Harvey Leonard|date=2026-06-10|access-date=2026-07-01}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Dustin Jacoby]] and former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Light Heavyweight Championship|LFA Light Heavyweight Champion]] Uran Satybaldiev was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://open.kg/en/news/sports-news/99957-opredelilsja-sledujuschij-sopernik-urana-satybaldieva-v-ufc.html|title=The next opponent of Uran Satybaldiev in the UFC has been determined|publisher=open.kg|author=Natalia Markova|date=2026-05-26|access-date=2026-07-09}} However, Satybaldiev withdrew for undisclosed reasons and was replaced by undefeated promotional newcomer Muhammad Saidov.{{Cite web|url=https://heavy.com/sports/ufc/dustin-jacoby-newcomer-ufc-abu-dhabi/|title=Dustin Jacoby takes on promotional newcomer at UFC Abu Dhabi|publisher=heavy.com|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2026-07-08|access-date=2026-07-09}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Bantamweight Championship|LFA Bantamweight Champion]] Muin Gafurov and promotional newcomer [[Abdul Hussein (fighter)|Abdul Hussein]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://heavy.com/sports/ufc/highly-touted-prospect-makes-ufc-debut/|title=Highly touted prospect makes UFC debut in Abu Dhabi|publisher=heavy.com|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2026-06-04|access-date=2026-07-09}} However, Gafurov withdrew due to a leg injury and was replaced by [[Cody Gibson]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.irishstar.com/sport/other-sports/white-ufc-fight-night-replacement-37399732|title=Dana White scrambles for replacement after UFC star forced to pull out of fight|publisher=irishstar.com|author=Tom Malley|date=2026-07-07|access-date=2026-07-09}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.iltalehti.fi/kamppailulajit/a/b2986d63-e6fe-4024-80cd-33241a30a096|title=Tässä on Suomen UFC-tähden uusi vastustaja!|publisher=iltalehti.fi|author=Viljami Kalmari and Ilari Savonen|date=2026-07-09|access-date=2026-07-10}} {{in lang|fi}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Islam Dulatov]] and [[Wellington Turman]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/06/01/ufc-abu-dhabi-main-event-magomed-ankalaev-vs-khalil-rountree/90217726007/|title=UFC Abu Dhabi main event features Magomed Ankalaev vs. Khalil Rountree|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2026-06-01|access-date=2026-07-25}} However just hours before it took place, Dulatov withdrew due to illness and the matchup was cancelled.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/501125/ufc-abu-dhabi-loses-fight-hours-before-event|title=UFC Abu Dhabi loses fight hours before 263]] in June 2021. featherweight bout between former interim [[UFC Featherweight Championship|UFC Featherweight Champion]] [[Yair Rodríguez]] and [[Jean Silva]] was scheduled to headline the event.{{Cite 24, 2026, at the [[Etihad Arena]] in [[Abu Dhabi]], United Arab Emirates.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sportsnet.ca/ufc/article/ufc-returning-to-abu-dhabi-for-ufc-333-in-october/|title=UFC returning to for UFC 333 on Oct. 24|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2026-07-25|access-date=2026-07-26}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's 24th visit to Abu Dhabi and its first since [[UFC Fight Night: Ankalaev vs. Guskov]] in July 2026. It was also the eleventh numbered event held in the city, following [[UFC 321]] in 12th October 2025.\n\nA [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] bout between reigning two-time champion [[Alexander Volkanovski]] and undefeated contender [[Movsar Evloev]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.telecomasia.net/news/mma/volkanovski-evloev-title-fight-ufc-333/|title=Volkanovski vs Evloev between reigning two-time champion [[Petr Yan]] and former champion [[Merab Dvalishvili]] took place as the co-main event.{{Cite defend their UFC belts at UFC 333|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Jordan Ellis|date=2026-08-09|access-date=2026-08-10}} The pair first met in March 2023 at [[UFC Fight Night: Yan vs. Dvalishvili]], where Dvalishvili defeated Yan via unanimous decision.{{Cite web|title=UFC Las Vegas: Merab Dvalishvili dominates ex-champ Petr Yan in standout performance|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2023/03/11/ufc-las-vegas-petr-yan-vs-merab-dvalishvili/|date=2023-03-11|access-date=2026-08-10|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson}} Their second meeting took place in December 2025 at [[UFC 323]], where Yan defeated Dvalishvili to reclaim the bantamweight title, ending the latter's 14-fight winning streak at the time.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2025/12/07/ufc-323-petr-yan-dethrones-merab-dvalishvili-in-rematch/|title=UFC 323: Petr Yan Dethrones Merab Dvalishvili in Rematch|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2025-12-07|access-date=2026-08-10}}\n\nA featherweight bout between [[Arnold Allen]] and [[Aaron Pico]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite women's strawweight non-title fight limit. [[UFC 333|UFC 333: Volkanovski vs. Evloev]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Buckley vs. Malott''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 291''''') was a mixed martial arts event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on October 17, 2026, at [[Rogers Place]] in [[Edmonton]], [[Alberta]], Canada.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/07/19/ufc-edmonton-fight-card-announcement-canada/90974963007/|title=UFC announces second Edmonton card in less than two years|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2026-07-19|access-date=2026-07-20}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's fourth visit to Edmonton and first since [[UFC Fight Night: Moreno vs. Albazi]] in November 2024.\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Joaquin Buckley]] and [[Mike Malott]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sportsnet.ca/ufc/article/canadas-mike-malott-to-headline-ufc-fight-night-in-edmonton/|title=Canada's Mike Malott to headline UFC Fight Night in Edmonton|publisher=sportsnet.ca|author=Staff|date=2026-07-28|access-date=2026-07-29}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between [[Erin Blanchfield]] and [[Jasmine Jasudavicius]] served as the co-main event.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2026/07/23/erin-blanchfield-jasmine-jasudavicius-shifts-from-ufc-330-targeted-edmonton/|title=Erin Blanchfield vs. Jasmine Jasudavicius shifts from UFC 330, targeted for Edmonton|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2026-07-23|access-date=2026-07-28}} They were initially scheduled to compete at [[UFC 330]] in August, but Blanchfield withdrew due to an injury.{{Cite web|url=https://boxingnews.com/news/erin-blanchfield-jasmine-jasudavicius-ufc-330|title=Erin Blanchfield vs. Jasmine Jasudavicius set for UFC 330|publisher=boxingnews.com|author=Tom Rashid|date=2026-06-18|access-date=2026-07-27}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/07/23/ufc-330-loses-top-contender-bout-philadelphia-fight-card/91022863007/|title=UFC 330 loses Erin Blanchfield vs. Jasmine Jasudavicius|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2026-07-27|access-date=2026-07-23}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between former [[UFC Women's Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[Katlyn Cerminara]] and former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Women's Flyweight Championship|LFA Women's Flyweight Champion]] Jamey-Lyn Horth was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/08/10/ufc-edmonton-four-new-fights-added-october-event/91249622007/|title=UFC Fight Night 291 adds four matchups to Edmonton event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matthew Wells|date=2026-08-10|access-date=2026-09-28}} However, Horth had to withdraw due to an injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer Mackenzie Stiller.{{Cite web|url=https://www.squamishchief.com/local-sports/update-squamishs-jamey-lyn-horth-forced-to-withdraw-from-ufc-fight-due-to-injury-12697243|title=Update: Squamish's Jamey-Lyn Horth scheduled at the event, but Sutherland pulled out and was replaced by fellow promotional newcomer Joel Faglier.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sportsnet.ca/ufc/article/faglier-to-replace-sutherland-at-ufc-edmonton/|title=Faglier to replace Sutherland at UFC Edmonton|publisher=sportsnet.ca|author=Staff|date=2026-09-30|access-date=2026-10-02}}\n\nIn addition, a middleweight bout between Julien Leblanc and Gilbert Urbina was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://ici.radio-canada.ca/nouvelle/2276116/julien-leblanc-marc-andre-barriault-ufc-edmonton|title=Un rêve se réalisera pour des Gatinois lors du UFC Fight Night|publisher=ici.radio-canada.ca|author=Martin Comtois|date=2026-08-19|access-date=2026-10-01}} {{in lang|fr}} However, Urbina withdrew for undisclosed reasons and was replaced by promotional newcomer Nick Galanti.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/10/01/ufc-edmonton-nick-galanti-vs-julien-leblanc-middleweight-mma-fight-booking-gilbert-urbina-out/92041488007/|title=Recent quick DWCS place on October 3, 2026, at the [[Delta Center]] in [[Salt Lake City]], Utah, United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sportsnet.ca/ufc/article/ufc-332-heading-to-salt-lake-city/|title=UFC 332 heading to Salt Lake City|publisher=sportsnet.ca|author=Staff|date=2026-08-08|access-date=2026-08-09}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's fifth visit to Salt Lake City and first since [[UFC 307]] in October 2024. This was the first time the entire main card of a numbered event aired on [[CBS]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/505923/natalia-silva-vs-wang-cong-undisputed-title-fight-set-for-ufc-332-main-event|title=Natalia Silva vs. Wang Cong undisputed title fight set for UFC 332 main event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2026-09-05|access-date=2026-09-06}}\n\nA [[UFC Women's Flyweight Championship]] bout for the vacant title between [[Natália Silva (fighter)|Natália Silva]] and [[Wang Cong]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://heavy.com/sports/ufc/vacant-title-fight-headlines-ufc-332/|title=Vacant title fight headlines UFC 332 in Salt Lake City|publisher=heavy.com|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2026-09-05|access-date=2026-09-06}} The original championship bout was supposed to be between current two-time champion [[Valentina Shevchenko]] and Silva, which was initially scheduled to serve as the co-main event and later moved to the main event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com.br/mma/ufc/artigo/_/id/17144601/ufc-define-proxima-luta-de-natalia-silva-e-brasileira-ganha-chance-pelo-cinturao|title=UFC define próxima luta de Natália Silva e brasileira ganha chance pelo cinturão|publisher=espn.com.br|author=Davi Garcia and Higor Ciconello|date=2026-08-20|access-date=2026-08-21}} {{in lang|pt-br}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.si.com/fannation/mma/news/valentina-shevchenko-could-surpass-amanda-nunes-ufc-332-title-fight|title=Valentina Shevchenko could surpass Amanda Nunes in reported UFC 332 title fight|publisher=si.com|author=Drew Beaupre|date=2026-08-20|access-date=2026-08-21}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.telecomasia.net/news/mma/shevchenko-vs-silva-fight-set-for-ufc-332-on-october-3/|title=Shevchenko vs. Silva fight set for UFC 332 on October 3|publisher=telecomasia.net|author=Telecom Asia Staff|date=2026-08-19|access-date=2026-08-21}} However, Shevchenko withdrew due to a ligament injury to her back and shoulder that will leave her sidelined for a year, which led to the cancellation of the bout and the title being vacated.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/09/05/ufc-332-natalia-silva-vs-wang-cong-for-interim-title-valentina-shevchenko/91575215007/|title=Natalia Silva vs. Wang Cong flyweight web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/505714/ufc-332-adds-several-new-bouts-including-johnny-walkers-heavyweight-debut|title=UFC 332 adds several new bouts, including Johnny Walker's heavyweight knockouts in a single UFC event]], surpassing [[UFC Fight Night: Imavov vs. Borralho]].\n\nThe event also broke the modern UFC record for [[List_of_UFC_records#Most_first-round_knockouts_-_In_a_single_event|most first-round knockouts in a single event]] with nine.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/50095968/ufc-332-sees-record-9-first-round-knockouts-11-total-finishes|title=UFC 332 sees record 9 first-round knockouts, 11 total consecutive knockouts in a modern-era UFC event with eight and the [[List_of_UFC_records#Most_knockdowns_-_In_a_single_event|most knockdowns landed in a single event]] with sixteen.{{Cite Nolan]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC 334|UFC 334: Gane vs. Hokit]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Cortes-Acosta vs. Walker''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 293''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 124''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on November 7, 2026, at the [[UFC Apex|Meta Apex]] in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas Valley]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/08/26/ufc-meta-apex-event-approvals/91468590007/|title=UFC officially adds four more Meta APEX events to 2026 schedule|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2026-08-26|access-date=2026-08-26}}\n\n==Background==\nA welterweight bout between former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Welterweight Championship|LFA Welterweight Champion]] [[Gabriel Bonfim]] and [[Sean Brady (fighter)|Sean Brady]] was scheduled to headline the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.thescore.com/mma/news/3580479/brady-bonfim-to-headline-nov-7-ufc-event|title=Brady-Bonfim to headline Nov. 7 UFC event|publisher=thescore.com|author=Nick Baldwin|date=2026-08-16|access-date=2026-08-16}} However, on October 29, Brady withdrew due to a staph infection in his knee, resulting in the cancellation of the bout.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/518348/sean-brady-out-of-ufc-vegas-124-main-event-against-gabriel-bonfim|title=Sean Brady out of UFC Vegas 124 main event against Gabriel Bonfim|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2026-10-29|access-date=2026-10-29}}{{Cite cancellation|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2026-10-29|access-date=2026-10-30}} In turn, a non-scheduled heavyweight bout between former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Heavyweight Championship|LFA Heavyweight Champion]] [[Waldo Cortes-Acosta]] and [[Johnny Walker (fighter)|Johnny Walker]] was subsequently booked as the event's new headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/ufc-fight-announcements/482485/waldo-cortes-acosta-vs-johnny-walker-now-headlines-ufc-vegas-124|title=Report: Waldo Cortes-Acosta vs Johnny Walker now headlines UFC Vegas 124|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Alexander Behunin|date=2026-10-29|access-date=2026-10-30}}\n\nA women's strawweight bout between former [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]] challenger (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Joanna vs. Team Cláudia]] strawweight winner) [[Tatiana Suarez]] and former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Strawweight Championship|Invicta FC Strawweight Champion]] (also former fellow strawweight title challenger) [[Virna Jandiroba]] is scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/09/11/virna-jandiroba-vs-tatiana-suarez-booked-again-for-ufc-vegas-124/91713281007/|title=Virna Jandiroba vs. Tatiana Suarez booked (again) for UFC Vegas 124|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2026-09-11|access-date=2026-09-12}} They were originally scheduled to compete at [[UFC on ESPN: Sandhagen vs. Font]] in August 2023, but Jandiroba had to withdraw due to a knee injury.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2023/5/18/23729392/tatiana-suarez-draws-virna-jandiroba-for-strawweight-return-in-august-ufc|title=Tatiana Suarez draws Virna Jandiroba for strawweight return in [[UFC 310]] in December 2024, but Suarez had to withdraw due to an unspecified health issue.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/9/17/24247696/tatiana-suarez-vs-virna-jandiroba-set-for-ufc-310-in-december|title=Tatiana Suarez vs. Virna Jandiroba set for UFC 310 in Jandiroba|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2024-10-21|access-date=2026-09-11}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between Alvin Hines and promotional newcomer Gabriel Lorenço was scheduled for this event. web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/tickets-sale-october-10-october-31-and-november-7-ufc-fight-night-events-meta-apex|title=Tickets On Sale For UFC Fight Night Events At Meta Walker]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Procházka vs. Stirling]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 334: Gane vs. Hokit''''' (branded as '''''Polymarket UFC 334''''' for sponsorship reasons) was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on November 14, 2026, at [[Madison Square Garden]] in [[New York City]], [[New York (state)|New York]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://sports.yahoo.com/news/ufc-334-335-announced-dates-010404268.html|title=UFC announces 2 major shows and one has a big sponsor added to the title|publisher=sports.yahoo.com|author=Brian Mazique|date=2026-08-23|access-date=2026-08-23}}\n\n==Background==\nThis event marked the promotion's 13th visit to New York City and first since [[UFC 322]] in November 2025.\n\nA [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Ciryl Gane]] and undefeated contender [[Josh Hokit]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/511102/ciryl-gane-vs-josh-hokit-kayla-harrison-vs-amanda-nunes-title-fights-set-for-ufc-334|title=Ciryl Gane vs. Josh Hokit, Kayla Harrison vs. Amanda Nunes title fights set for UFC 334|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2026-09-20|access-date=2026-09-20}} On September 14, 2026, then-reigning champion [[Tom Aspinall]] vacated his heavyweight title due to ongoing complications from the eye injury he sustained via an illegal poke during his title defense against Gane in October 2025 at [[UFC 321]], which ended in a no contest.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2025/10/25/tom-aspinall-fuming-after-ufc-321-headliner-ends-early-due-to-ciryl-gane-eye-poke/|title=Tom Aspinall fuming after UFC 321 headliner ends early due to Ciryl Gane eye poke|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2025-10-25|access-date=2026-09-20}}{{Cite web|url=https://heavy.com/sports/ufc/tom-aspinall-vacates-ufc-heavyweight-title/|title=Tom Aspinall vacates UFC heavyweight title|publisher=heavy.com|author=Adam Martin|date=2026-09-14|access-date=2026-09-20}} Gane was originally the interim champion after defeating former [[List of UFC champions#Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight]] and two-time [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship|UFC Light Heavyweight]] champion [[Alex Pereira]] on June 14, 2026 at [[UFC Freedom 250]].{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2026/06/15/ufc-freedom-250-ciryl-gane-lights-up-devastates-alex-pereira-spoiling-heavyweight-debut/|title=UFC Freedom 250: Ciryl Gane lights up, devastates Alex Pereira, spoiling heavyweight debut|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2026-06-15|access-date=2026-09-20}} Although CEO [[Dana White]] originally said Gane would not be elevated to undisputed champion, White later clarified that he misspoke and confirmed that Gane had indeed been promoted and would enter this bout as the defending champion.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/511027/dana-white-says-ciryl-gane-wont-be-promoted-to-undisputed-champion-hes-still-got-to-fight|title=Dana White says Ciryl Gane won't be promoted to undisputed champion: 'He's still got to fight'|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Jed Meshew|date=2026-09-19|access-date=2026-09-20}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.independent.co.uk/sport/ufc/ciryl-gane-next-fight-josh-hokit-tom-aspinall-b3053028.html|title=Ciryl Gane crowned UFC heavyweight champion and defends title vs controversial Josh Hokit|publisher=independent.co.uk|author=Alex Pattle|date=2026-09-20|access-date=2026-09-20}}\n\nA [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]] bout between the reigning champion [[Kayla Harrison]] (who is also a [[Judo at the 2012 Summer Olympics – Women's 78 kg|2012]] and [[Judo at the 2016 Summer Olympics – Women's 78 kg|2016 Olympic gold medalist in judo]]; and a two-time [[Professional Fighters League|PFL]] women's lightweight tournament champion) and former two-time champion [[Amanda Nunes]], who also held the [[UFC Women's Featherweight Championship]], co-headlined the event. Nunes, who is widely considered the greatest women's fighter in UFC history, returned for her first bout since [[UFC 289]] in June 2023, where she announced her retirement following a successful title defense against [[Irene Aldana]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/mixed-martial-arts/articles/c6e3dw87pyjeo|title=The challenge that brought Nunes out of retirement|publisher=bbc.com|author=Paul Battison|date=2026-11-14|access-date=2026-11-14}} The bout was originally scheduled for [[UFC 324]] in January 2026, but Harrison withdrew from the bout after being diagnosed with herniated discs in her neck, which required surgery.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/466164/report-kayla-harrison-out-of-ufc-324-fight-against-amanda-nunes-postponed-to-later-date|title=Kayla Harrison out of UFC 324, fight against Amanda Nunes postponed to later date|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2026-01-14|access-date=2026-09-18}} The bout was also reportedly linked to [[UFC 331]], which took place in September, but it did not materialize for undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://bloodyelbow.com/2026/08/01/arman-tsarukyan-reportedly-wont-be-allowed-to-challenge-for-charles-oliveiras-bmf-title-at-ufc-331/|title=Arman Tsarukyan reportedly won't be allowed to challenge for Charles Oliveira's BMF title at UFC 331|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Harvey Leonard|date=2026-08-01|access-date=2026-09-18}} [[Joselyne Edwards]] served as backup and potential replacement for this fight.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/518223/joselyne-edwards-to-serve-as-backup-fighter-for-kayla-harrison-vs-amanda-nunes-at-ufc-334|title=Joselyne Edwards to serve as backup fighter for Kayla Harrison vs. Amanda Nunes at UFC 334|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2026-10-28|access-date=2026-10-28}}\n\nUndefeated prospects Donte Johnson and Baisangur Susurkaev were scheduled to fight at the event in a middleweight bout.{{Cite web|url=https://heavy.com/sports/ufc/ufc-334-gane-hokit-officially-announced/|title=UFC 334: Gane vs. Hokit officially announced|publisher=heavy.com|author=Adam Martin|date=2026-09-19|access-date=2026-09-20}} However, Susurkaev withdrew from the bout for unknown reasons and was replaced by [[César Almeida (fighter)|César Almeida]] in a catchweight of 190 pounds.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/513222/ufc-334-loses-top-middleweight-prospect-cesar-almeida-steps-in|title=UFC 334 loses top middleweight prospect, Cesar Almeida steps in|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2026-09-29|access-date=2026-09-30}} Almeida was fined 30 percent of his purse for asking for the bout to be moved to a catchweight bout.\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Nazim Sadykhov]] and Jefferson Nascimento was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/First-UFC-334-fight-revealed-for-Madison-Square-Garden-card-202638|title=First UFC 334 fight revealed for Madison Square Garden card|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Sayan Nag|date=2026-09-03|access-date=2026-09-03}} However, Nascimento withdrew due to an injury, and Sadykhov subsequently withdrew due to a lingering injury of his own, resulting in the bout's cancellation.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/UFC-334-loses-lightweight-bout-after-both-fighters-suffer-injuries-203118|title=UFC 334 loses lightweight bout after both fighters suffer injuries|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Sayan Nag|date=2026-10-08|access-date=2026-10-08}}\n\n[[Adrian Yañez]] and Juan Díaz were expected to meet in a bantamweight bout at the preliminary card.{{Cite web|url=https://heavy.com/sports/ufc/bantamweight-barnburner-added-to-ufc-334/|title=Bantamweight Barnburner Added to UFC 334 in New York|publisher=heavy.com|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2026-09-15|access-date=2026-09-15}} However, Yañez had to withdraw in mid October due to injury and was replaced by [[Alateng Heili]].{{Cite web|url=https://sports.ndtv.com/us/us-streamers/ufc-334-fight-cancellation-adrian-yanez-withdraws-from-juan-diaz-bout-as-ufc-has-yet-to-name-replacement-12198639|title=UFC 334 fight cancellation: Adrian Yanez withdraws from Juan Diaz bout as UFC has yet to name replacement|publisher=sports.ndtv.com|author=NDTV Sports Desk|date=2026-10-17|access-date=2026-10-18}}{{Cite web|url=https://heavy.com/sports/ufc/ufc-334-card-gets-late-booking/|title=UFC 334 card gets late booking|publisher=heavy.com|author=Adam Martin|date=2026-11-06|access-date=2026-11-07}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, two fighters missed weight:{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/11/13/ufc-334-official-weigh-in-results-live-stream-gane-hokit-harrison-nunes/92634095007/|title=UFC 334 weigh-in results: Title fights set, but two misses in New York|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2026-11-13|access-date=2026-11-13}} \n*[[Terrance McKinney]] weighed in at 160.2 pounds, 4.2 pounds over the lightweight non-title fight limit.\n*Díaz weighed in at 136.4 pounds, 0.4 pounds over the bantamweight non-title fight limit.\n\nBoth bouts proceeded at catchweight. The fighters were fined a percentage of their individual purses which went to their opponents [[Jim Miller (fighter)|Jim Miller]] (30 percent) and Alateng (20 percent), respectively.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/520891/ufc-334-weigh-in-results-1-fighter-misses-weight-by-over-4-pounds-championship-bouts-set|title=UFC 334 weigh-in results: 1 fighter misses weight by over 4 pounds, championship bouts set|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K.
